1392 — sled one leg calf press on leg press

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1392 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A one-leg calf press on a leg press sled using only your toes. It trains each calf separately under a supported load.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Adjust the seat of the leg press machine so that your knees are slightly bent when your feet are on the sled. Sit on the machine with your back against the backrest and your feet on the sled, shoulder-width apart. Place your toes and the balls of your feet on the sled, keeping your heels off.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1392.json#/instructions/0, 1392.json#/instructions/1, 1392.json#/instructions/2

Arms Hands

Rest relaxed hands beside the thighs until the source's arm movement begins; use documented handles or loads when present.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 1392.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

Adjust the seat of the leg press machine so that your knees are slightly bent when your feet are on the sled. Sit on the machine with your back against the backrest and your feet on the sled, shoulder-width apart. Place your toes and the balls of your feet on the sled, keeping your heels off.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1392.json#/instructions/0, 1392.json#/instructions/1, 1392.json#/instructions/2

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: no additional prop is established. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1392.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Adjust the seat of the leg press machine so that your knees are slightly bent when your feet are on the sled. 1392.json#/instructions/0
  2. Sit on the machine with your back against the backrest and your feet on the sled, shoulder-width apart. 1392.json#/instructions/1
  3. Place your toes and the balls of your feet on the sled, keeping your heels off. 1392.json#/instructions/2
  4. Push the sled forward by extending your ankles, keeping your knees slightly bent. 1392.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pause for a moment at the top, then slowly lower the sled back down by flexing your ankles. 1392.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 1392.json#/instructions/5

1393 — smith one leg floor calf raise

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1393 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: source_defined_support.

A one-leg calf raise lifting a smith machine bar from the floor. It works each calf on its own under a guided bar path.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Position yourself on the floor under the smith machine bar, facing away from the machine. Place the balls of your feet on a raised surface, such as a weight plate or block. Position the smith machine bar across your lower legs, just above your ankles. Hold onto the bar with your hands for stability.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1393.json#/instructions/0, 1393.json#/instructions/1, 1393.json#/instructions/2, 1393.json#/instructions/3

Arms Hands

Hold onto the bar with your hands for stability.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1393.json#/instructions/3

Legs Feet

Place the balls of your feet on a raised surface, such as a weight plate or block. Position the smith machine bar across your lower legs, just above your ankles.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1393.json#/instructions/1, 1393.json#/instructions/2

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Weight plate, Raised platform/deficit block, Foot/calf support block or wedge. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1393.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Position yourself on the floor under the smith machine bar, facing away from the machine. 1393.json#/instructions/0
  2. Place the balls of your feet on a raised surface, such as a weight plate or block. 1393.json#/instructions/1
  3. Position the smith machine bar across your lower legs, just above your ankles. 1393.json#/instructions/2
  4. Hold onto the bar with your hands for stability. 1393.json#/instructions/3
  5. Raise your heels off the ground by extending your ankles, lifting your body up. 1393.json#/instructions/4
  6. Pause at the top of the movement, then slowly lower your heels back down to the starting position. 1393.json#/instructions/5
  7. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 1393.json#/instructions/6

  • Hold onto the bar with your hands for stability. Step 4; 1393.json#/instructions/3

1394 — smith reverse calf raises

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1394 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A smith machine calf raise done with the bar in front of you. It targets the calves and the muscles around the shin.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Adjust the smith machine bar to a height just below your shoulders. Stand facing the bar with your feet hip-width apart and toes pointing forward. Place the balls of your feet on the edge of a step or platform, with your heels hanging off. Hold onto the bar for support.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1394.json#/instructions/0, 1394.json#/instructions/1, 1394.json#/instructions/2, 1394.json#/instructions/3

Arms Hands

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 1394.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

Stand facing the bar with your feet hip-width apart and toes pointing forward. Place the balls of your feet on the edge of a step or platform, with your heels hanging off.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1394.json#/instructions/1, 1394.json#/instructions/2

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Aerobic step/riser, Raised platform/deficit block. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1394.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Adjust the smith machine bar to a height just below your shoulders. 1394.json#/instructions/0
  2. Stand facing the bar with your feet hip-width apart and toes pointing forward. 1394.json#/instructions/1
  3. Place the balls of your feet on the edge of a step or platform, with your heels hanging off. 1394.json#/instructions/2
  4. Hold onto the bar for support. 1394.json#/instructions/3
  5. Raise your heels as high as possible, lifting your body up onto your toes. 1394.json#/instructions/4
  6. Pause for a moment at the top, then slowly lower your heels back down to the starting position. 1394.json#/instructions/5
  7. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 1394.json#/instructions/6

  • Hold onto the bar for support. Step 4; 1394.json#/instructions/3

1395 — smith seated one leg calf raise

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1395 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A seated one-leg calf raise using a smith machine. The bent knee puts the focus on the soleus under the calf.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on the machine with your back against the pad and your feet on the footrest. Place one leg on the footrest and keep the other leg off the footrest.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1395.json#/instructions/0, 1395.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Rest relaxed hands beside the thighs until the source's arm movement begins; use documented handles or loads when present.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 1395.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1395.json#/instructions/0, 1395.json#/instructions/1

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: no additional prop is established. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1395.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit on the machine with your back against the pad and your feet on the footrest. 1395.json#/instructions/0
  2. Place one leg on the footrest and keep the other leg off the footrest. 1395.json#/instructions/1
  3. Using your calf muscles, raise your heel as high as possible. 1395.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause for a moment at the top, then slowly lower your heel back down to the starting position. 1395.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions, then switch legs and repeat. 1395.json#/instructions/4
Extracted position and contact cues

1396 — smith toe raise

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1396 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A smith machine toe raise where you rise onto the balls of your feet. It builds the calves and ankle strength.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Adjust the smith machine bar to a height that allows you to comfortably stand with your feet flat on the ground. Position yourself under the bar with your shoulders directly below it and your feet shoulder-width apart. Place the balls of your feet on a raised platform or weight plates, with your heels hanging off the edge. Grasp the bar with an overhand grip, slightly wider than shoulder-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1396.json#/instructions/0, 1396.json#/instructions/1, 1396.json#/instructions/2, 1396.json#/instructions/3

Arms Hands

Grasp the bar with an overhand grip, slightly wider than shoulder-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1396.json#/instructions/3

Legs Feet

Adjust the smith machine bar to a height that allows you to comfortably stand with your feet flat on the ground. Position yourself under the bar with your shoulders directly below it and your feet shoulder-width apart. Place the balls of your feet on a raised platform or weight plates, with your heels hanging off the edge.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1396.json#/instructions/0, 1396.json#/instructions/1, 1396.json#/instructions/2

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1396.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Adjust the smith machine bar to a height that allows you to comfortably stand with your feet flat on the ground. 1396.json#/instructions/0
  2. Position yourself under the bar with your shoulders directly below it and your feet shoulder-width apart. 1396.json#/instructions/1
  3. Place the balls of your feet on a raised platform or weight plates, with your heels hanging off the edge. 1396.json#/instructions/2
  4. Grasp the bar with an overhand grip, slightly wider than shoulder-width apart. 1396.json#/instructions/3
  5. Engage your core and keep your back straight throughout the exercise. 1396.json#/instructions/4
  6. Slowly raise your heels as high as possible, lifting your body weight onto the balls of your feet. 1396.json#/instructions/5
  7. Pause for a moment at the top, then slowly lower your heels back down to the starting position. 1396.json#/instructions/6
  8. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 1396.json#/instructions/7

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supine.

Lie on your back with your heels on a stability ball and curl one leg toward you while kicking diagonally. It works the hamstrings, glutes and core.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Start by lying on your back with your legs extended and your heels resting on top of the stability ball. Place your arms by your sides for stability.

  3. Engage your glutes and core muscles to lift your hips off the ground, creating a straight line from your shoulders to your heels. 1417.json#/instructions/2

  6. Slowly return your right leg to the starting position, maintaining control and stability. 1417.json#/instructions/5
  7. Repeat the movement with your left leg, alternating sides for the desired number of repetitions. 1417.json#/instructions/6
